# ============================================================
# PART 41 — PRODUCTION ARCHITECTURE & AI AGENT SYSTEM
# ============================================================

# 41.1 PRODUCTION ARCHITECTURE
#
# TriLink Global production architecture must remain centred on:
#
#                 TRILINK GLOBAL
#                       |
#                       v
#             PLATFORMSIFY PLATFORM
#                       |
#                       v
#             PLATFORMSIFY CEO DASHBOARD
#                       |
#        +--------------+--------------+
#        |              |              |
#        v              v              v
#     AI LAYER       DATA LAYER     INTEGRATIONS
#        |              |              |
#        +--------------+--------------+
#                       |
#                       v
#              TRILINK OPERATIONS
#
# The CEO Dashboard remains the executive command centre.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.2 AI AGENT ARCHITECTURE
# ============================================================
#
# TriLink AI should use specialised agents rather than one
# uncontrolled general-purpose agent.
#
# CORE AGENTS:
#
# 1. CEO INTELLIGENCE AGENT
#    Produces executive summaries, KPIs, risks and opportunities.
#
# 2. SALES AGENT
#    Manages leads, prospects, follow-ups and sales pipeline.
#
# 3. CUSTOMER AGENT
#    Handles onboarding, support and customer communication.
#
# 4. SUPPLIER AGENT
#    Manages supplier discovery, qualification and communication.
#
# 5. TRADE AGENT
#    Supports quotations, orders, documentation and transactions.
#
# 6. LOGISTICS AGENT
#    Supports shipment planning, tracking and exception handling.
#
# 7. ENERGY AGENT
#    Supports energy opportunities, suppliers, buyers and projects.
#
# 8. FINANCE AGENT
#    Produces forecasts, reconciliations, financial alerts and
#    management reports.
#
# 9. COMPLIANCE AGENT
#    Checks documents, requirements, risks and regulatory workflows.
#
# 10. DATA / ANALYTICS AGENT
#     Produces business intelligence and performance analysis.
#
# 11. SYSTEM OPERATIONS AGENT
#     Monitors system health, jobs, failures and operational errors.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.3 AGENT COMMUNICATION
# ============================================================
#
# Agents must not create uncontrolled agent-to-agent actions.
#
# Agent communication must use:
# - defined workflows
# - defined permissions
# - structured data
# - traceable requests
# - audit records
#
# High-risk agent actions must be escalated to the CEO approval layer.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.4 DATA SOURCES
# ============================================================
#
# Approved data sources may include:
#
# - TriLink CRM
# - customers
# - suppliers
# - transactions
# - shipments
# - financial records
# - energy projects
# - documents
# - approved external data
# - operational telemetry
#
# AI must distinguish:
#
# VERIFIED DATA
# ESTIMATED DATA
# USER-PROVIDED DATA
# EXTERNAL DATA
# AI-GENERATED RECOMMENDATIONS
#
# ============================================================
# 41.5 KNOWLEDGE BASE
# ============================================================
#
# TriLink should maintain a controlled company knowledge base
# containing:
#
# - company policies
# - operating procedures
# - approved pricing rules
# - supplier requirements
# - customer requirements
# - trade procedures
# - logistics procedures
# - energy procedures
# - compliance documents
# - approved templates
# - executive decisions
#
# AI may use the knowledge base only according to its permissions.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.6 AI MEMORY
# ============================================================
#
# AI memory must be separated into:
#
# SHORT-TERM
# Current workflow and task context.
#
# OPERATIONAL
# Approved customer, supplier and transaction information.
#
# ORGANISATIONAL
# Company procedures, policies and decisions.
#
# EXECUTIVE
# Approved strategic information.
#
# Sensitive information must have restricted access.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.7 FAILURE HANDLING
# ============================================================
#
# If an AI agent fails:
#
# 1. Record failure.
# 2. Stop unsafe continuation.
# 3. Retry only within approved limits.
# 4. Escalate persistent failure.
# 5. Preserve the original transaction state.
# 6. Notify the appropriate operational owner.
# 7. Record the final resolution.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.8 NO SINGLE POINT OF AI FAILURE
# ============================================================
#
# Critical workflows must have:
#
# - failure detection
# - retry policy
# - fallback procedure
# - human escalation
# - transaction recovery
#
# AI automation must never compromise the underlying business
# transaction.
#
# ============================================================
# 41.9 CEO DASHBOARD AGENT VIEW
# ============================================================
#
# Platformsify CEO Dashboard should provide:
#
# - active agents
# - agent status
# - tasks completed
# - tasks pending
# - failures
# - escalations
# - approvals
# - AI recommendations
# - financial impact
# - operational impact
#
# ============================================================
# 41.10 ARCHITECTURE RULE
# ============================================================
#
# DO NOT build a second executive command centre.
#
# If another TriLink application needs executive information,
# it must expose the information through the Platformsify CEO
# Dashboard architecture or approved integrations.
#
# Platformsify remains the technology operating layer.
#
# ============================================================
# PART 41 COMPLETE
# ============================================================

# ============================================================
# PART 43 — COMMERCIAL LAUNCH & REVENUE ENGINE
# ============================================================

# 43.1 COMMERCIAL OBJECTIVE
#
# TriLink must convert its technology and operational capability
# into real commercial transactions.
#
# PRIMARY OBJECTIVES:
# - acquire qualified customers
# - identify qualified suppliers
# - create trade opportunities
# - convert opportunities into transactions
# - manage logistics
# - generate recurring revenue
# - protect margins
# - build long-term commercial relationships
#
# ============================================================
# 43.2 REVENUE STREAMS
# ============================================================
#
# Potential revenue streams:
#
# 1. TRADE MARGIN
#    Margin generated through approved trading transactions.
#
# 2. LOGISTICS SERVICES
#    Fees or margins from logistics coordination.
#
# 3. SOURCING SERVICES
#    Fees for approved sourcing and procurement services.
#
# 4. TRADE MANAGEMENT
#    Fees for managing commercial transactions.
#
# 5. ENERGY SERVICES
#    Approved commissions, project fees or transaction margins.
#
# 6. B2B PLATFORM SERVICES
#    Future subscription or service revenue where commercially
#    appropriate.
#
# No revenue stream becomes active without appropriate commercial,
# legal and regulatory review.
#
# ============================================================
# 43.3 AI SALES ENGINE
# ============================================================
#

# ============================================================
# PART 44 — AUTONOMOUS AI OPERATIONS & CONTINUOUS IMPROVEMENT
# ============================================================

# 44.1 AI OPERATING PRINCIPLE
#
# TriLink should automate repetitive, measurable and low-risk work.
#
# Humans remain responsible for:
# - strategic authority
# - ownership
# - major financial decisions
# - legal commitments
# - regulatory decisions
# - high-risk exceptions
#
# ============================================================
# 44.2 AUTONOMOUS WORKFLOW CATEGORIES
# ============================================================
#
# AI may operate approved workflows for:
#
# - data classification
# - CRM updates
# - routine customer follow-ups
# - routine supplier follow-ups
# - report generation
# - KPI calculation
# - document preparation
# - shipment status monitoring
# - routine alerts
# - task prioritisation
# - internal summaries
# - approved scheduling
#
# ============================================================
# 44.3 HUMAN-IN-THE-LOOP
# ============================================================
#
# AI must escalate when:
#
# - confidence is low
# - information conflicts
# - transaction value exceeds limits
# - legal implications are unclear
# - compliance risk is detected
# - customer dispute occurs
# - supplier dispute occurs
# - unusual financial behaviour is detected
# - system integrity is uncertain
#
# ============================================================
# 44.4 AI CONFIDENCE
# ============================================================
#
# AI decisions should be classified:
#
# HIGH CONFIDENCE
# → approved workflow may continue.
#
# MEDIUM CONFIDENCE
# → additional verification required.
#
# LOW CONFIDENCE
# → human review required.
#
# AI must never manufacture confidence.
#
# ============================================================
# 44.5 CONTINUOUS IMPROVEMENT
# ============================================================
#
# AI performance must be reviewed using:
#
# - accuracy
# - completion rate
# - error rate
# - escalation rate
# - customer outcomes
# - supplier outcomes
# - financial outcomes
# - operational outcomes
#
# ============================================================
# 44.6 LEARNING CONTROL
# ============================================================
#
# AI may learn from approved operational outcomes.
#
# Production behaviour must not change unpredictably.
#
# Material changes to AI behaviour must be:
#
# - versioned
# - tested
# - reviewed
# - approved where required
# - auditable
#
# ============================================================
# 44.7 AI PERFORMANCE DASHBOARD
# ============================================================
#
# Platformsify CEO Dashboard should display:
#
# - agent uptime
# - tasks completed
# - tasks failed
# - tasks escalated
# - average response time
# - workflow accuracy
# - approval rate
# - automation rate
# - estimated time saved
# - operational impact
#
# ============================================================
# 44.8 AI COST CONTROL
# ============================================================
#
# Monitor:
#
# - AI usage
# - model/API costs
# - workflow frequency
# - unnecessary calls
# - failed/repeated calls
# - cost per workflow
# - cost per transaction
#
# AI automation must create economic value.
#
# ============================================================
# 44.9 AGENT VERSION CONTROL
# ============================================================
#
# Every production AI agent must have:
#
# - agent name
# - version
# - purpose
# - permissions
# - owner
# - deployment date
# - change history
# - rollback capability
#
# ============================================================
# 44.10 AUTOMATION MATURITY
# ============================================================
#
# LEVEL 1
# Manual operation.
#
# LEVEL 2
# AI-assisted operation.
#
# LEVEL 3
# AI executes approved routine tasks.
#
# LEVEL 4
# AI coordinates multiple approved workflows.
#
# LEVEL 5
# Highly automated operation with continuous human governance.
#
# TriLink should progress through these levels based on evidence,
# not assumptions.
#
# ============================================================
# 44.11 CONTINUOUS IMPROVEMENT LOOP
# ============================================================
#
# MEASURE
#   ↓
# ANALYSE
#   ↓
# IDENTIFY PROBLEM
#   ↓
# PROPOSE CHANGE
#   ↓
# TEST
#   ↓
# APPROVE
#   ↓
# DEPLOY
#   ↓
# MONITOR
#
# ============================================================
# PART 44 COMPLETE
# ============================================================

# ============================================================
# PART 46 — AI GOVERNANCE, AUDIT & EXECUTIVE CONTROL
# ============================================================

# TriLink operates with AI-first automation, but all AI activity
# remains governed by the Founder / Chairman / CEO.

# Platformsify CEO Dashboard is the SINGLE executive control centre.

# AI GOVERNANCE
# - every AI agent has a defined role
# - every agent has permission boundaries
# - every automated action is logged
# - financial actions require appropriate approval levels
# - legal/regulatory actions require human approval
# - high-risk decisions must escalate to the CEO
# - AI must never create authority it was not granted
# - AI must never bypass dashboard permissions
# - AI must never conceal failed actions
# - AI must never fabricate transactions, customers, suppliers,
#   shipments, documents, revenue or compliance records

# EXECUTIVE ESCALATION LEVELS

# LEVEL 1 — FULL AI AUTONOMY
# Routine approved operational tasks.
# Examples:
# - data entry
# - document classification
# - status updates
# - internal notifications
# - routine reporting
# - shipment monitoring
# - lead qualification
# - approved customer communications

# LEVEL 2 — AI + APPROVAL
# AI prepares the action; authorised human approval is required.
# Examples:
# - significant quotations
# - supplier onboarding
# - customer onboarding
# - unusual pricing
# - contractual commitments
# - significant payment requests

# LEVEL 3 — CEO ONLY
# Founder / Chairman / CEO approval required.
# Examples:
# - ownership changes
# - banking changes
# - major contracts
# - major investments
# - major debt
# - acquisitions
# - country expansion
# - strategic partnerships
# - major AI permission changes
# - legal/regulatory decisions

# AUDIT SYSTEM
# Maintain immutable records of:
# - who initiated an action
# - AI agent involved
# - date/time
# - action requested
# - action performed
# - approval status
# - financial value where applicable
# - result
# - errors
# - escalation
# - final decision

# CEO DASHBOARD MUST PROVIDE
# - live executive alerts
# - pending approvals
# - AI activity
# - financial overview
# - trade pipeline
# - logistics pipeline
# - energy pipeline
# - customer activity
# - supplier activity
# - risk alerts
# - compliance alerts
# - system health
# - audit history
# - strategic KPIs

# SECURITY PRINCIPLE
# No single AI process should have unrestricted access to the
# entire company infrastructure.

# Use least-privilege permissions and separate operational,
# financial, customer, supplier and administrative capabilities.

# END PART 46


# ============================================================
# PART 47 — BUSINESS CONTINUITY, DISASTER RECOVERY & SCALE
# ============================================================

# TriLink must be designed to continue operating safely if an
# individual service, AI agent, integration or deployment fails.

# BUSINESS CONTINUITY
# Critical business functions must have:
# - monitored service health
# - error detection
# - transaction logging
# - recoverable data
# - controlled retry mechanisms
# - clear escalation procedures
# - CEO visibility

# DATA PROTECTION
# Protect:
# - customers
# - suppliers
# - transactions
# - quotations
# - invoices
# - shipments
# - contracts
# - financial records
# - compliance records
# - AI audit logs
# - CEO decisions

# BACKUP PRINCIPLES
# - maintain reliable backups
# - test restoration
# - protect credentials
# - separate production from backup systems
# - prevent accidental deletion
# - maintain recovery documentation

# INCIDENT MANAGEMENT
# When a critical failure occurs:
#
# 1. Detect
# 2. Classify
# 3. Contain
# 4. Protect data
# 5. Notify the appropriate authority
# 6. Recover
# 7. Verify
# 8. Record the incident
# 9. Review the root cause
# 10. Implement corrective action

# AI FAILURE POLICY
# If an AI agent produces unreliable output:
# - stop the affected workflow
# - preserve the audit trail
# - prevent repeated harmful execution
# - escalate to the appropriate authority
# - restore the last known safe operating state

# SCALE ARCHITECTURE
# TriLink should be capable of expanding from:
#
# Phase A:
# Nepal → India → Bangladesh
#
# Phase B:
# additional South Asian trade corridors
#
# Phase C:
# wider Asian trade network
#
# Phase D:
# international trade and logistics network
#
# Energy operations can scale independently while remaining
# controlled through the same Platformsify CEO Dashboard.

# SCALE WITHOUT MANAGEMENT FRAGMENTATION
# Expansion must NOT create multiple competing CEO dashboards.
#
# New divisions, countries and operating units should report
# into the same executive control architecture.

# COMPANY OPERATING MODEL
#
# Founder / Chairman / CEO
#          │
#          ▼
# Platformsify CEO Dashboard
#          │
#    ┌─────┼─────┐
#    ▼     ▼     ▼
#  Trade Logistics Energy
#    │     │     │
#    └─────┼─────┘
#          ▼
#      AI OPERATIONS
#          │
#          ▼
# Customers / Suppliers / Partners

# FINAL SCALE PRINCIPLE
# Grow the business without losing:
# - executive control
# - financial discipline
# - compliance
# - auditability
# - security
# - operational visibility
# - customer trust

# END PART 47

# ============================================================
# PART 49 — FINANCE, RISK & COMPLIANCE CONTROL
# ============================================================

# PURPOSE
# Protect TriLink from financial, operational, legal,
# regulatory and counterparty risk.

# FINANCIAL CONTROL
# The CEO Dashboard must provide visibility over:
# - cash position
# - receivables
# - payables
# - revenue
# - gross profit
# - operating costs
# - committed expenditure
# - outstanding invoices
# - payment status
# - cash-flow forecast

# AI FINANCE FUNCTIONS
# AI may:
# - categorise transactions
# - reconcile approved records
# - identify anomalies
# - forecast cash flow
# - prepare management reports
# - identify overdue invoices
# - flag unusual transactions
#
# AI may NOT independently change bank details,
# authorise unrestricted payments or bypass approval controls.

# PAYMENT CONTROL
# High-risk financial actions require appropriate approval.
#
# Mandatory verification for:
# - new bank accounts
# - changed supplier bank details
# - unusual payment requests
# - large transactions
# - refunds
# - exceptional discounts
# - related-party transactions

# FRAUD PREVENTION
# Monitor for:
# - duplicate invoices
# - duplicate payments
# - unusual pricing
# - unusual account changes
# - suspicious customer behaviour
# - suspicious supplier behaviour
# - abnormal transaction patterns
# - identity inconsistencies

# RISK REGISTER
# Maintain a live risk register covering:
# - financial risk
# - supplier risk
# - customer risk
# - logistics risk
# - country risk
# - currency risk
# - regulatory risk
# - cybersecurity risk
# - operational risk
# - reputational risk
# - AI risk

# RISK LEVELS
# LOW
# Routine monitoring.
#
# MEDIUM
# Increased monitoring and management review.
#
# HIGH
# Escalation and approval before proceeding.
#
# CRITICAL
# Suspend affected workflow and escalate immediately.

# COMPLIANCE
# TriLink must comply with applicable:
# - company law
# - tax requirements
# - customs requirements
# - import/export requirements
# - sanctions requirements
# - anti-money-laundering requirements
# - data-protection requirements
# - sector-specific energy requirements
# - contractual requirements

# IMPORTANT
# AI provides operational assistance.
# Qualified human professionals remain responsible for
# specialist legal, tax, regulatory and accounting advice.

# DOCUMENT CONTROL
# Store controlled versions of:
# - contracts
# - invoices
# - purchase orders
# - quotations
# - shipping documents
# - compliance documents
# - supplier records
# - customer records
# - approvals
# - audit records

# CEO ALERTS
# Immediate executive alert for:
# - suspected fraud
# - critical compliance issue
# - major financial anomaly
# - significant failed payment
# - major supplier failure
# - major customer dispute
# - cybersecurity incident
# - material operational disruption

# RISK PRINCIPLE
# AI should prefer a safe escalation over an irreversible
# action when information is incomplete or contradictory.

# END PART 49
